### [non-spec.6.review-reliability.1]

- FACT: `pgtenant.InTx` runs the closure once with no retry, so a writer call inside the `credentialPools.Update` closure is never re-invoked by the store; Put/Delete idempotence matters only for client retries and Phase 4 re-runs — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/storage/pgtenant/pgtenant.go:76-102
- FACT: Phase 4 stops at the first failing eraser and a later pass re-runs the whole sequence; `pgstore.DeleteByTenant` is a plain `DELETE ... WHERE tenant_id`, so a re-run after a failed `Writer.DeleteTenant` deletes zero rows and retries the delete-collection — EVIDENCE: cmd/lenny-gateway/tenant_erasers.go:37-41, pkg/gateway/credentials/credentialpoolstore/pgstore/pgstore.go:271-292
- FACT: `credential_pools` is unique on (tenant_id, name) and `Create` maps 23505 to ErrAlreadyExists, so a soft-deleted pool is revived only by the bootstrap force-update closure (`pool.DeletedAt = time.Time{}`) — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/credentials/credentialpoolstore/pgstore/pgstore.go:95-98, pkg/gateway/externalapi/admin/bootstrap.go:407-422
- WATCHOUT: the current `SoftDelete` is idempotent (`WHERE deleted_at IS NULL`); CODE-4 step 6's `Update` closure re-sets `DeletedAt` on an already-deleted row unless guarded. `deleted_at` is an audit timestamp only, so not filed — EVIDENCE: pgstore.go:225-247, credentialpoolstore.go:156-170
- DECISION: returned no findings under the reliability lens — BECAUSE writer Put (Create then unconditional Update), Delete (NotFound→nil), and DeleteTenant are idempotent under client retry and Phase 4 re-run, every residue a crash or failover leaves is in rule 5's table, and the probe-under-lock deadline is already refuted — ALTERNATIVES: filing the seed revival of a soft-deleted pool with a same-id `lenny-cred-` ref (an active entry with an absent Secret); rejected because rule 5 row 2 covers that state and it fails closed

### [spec.1.review-reliability.1]
DECISION: no reliability finding on the staged spec edits. BECAUSE I traced each Lenny-written Secret operation through crash, Postgres failover, a lost response with a client retry, a partial multi-Delete failure, and a tenant-deletion Phase 4 retry, and every resulting state is a row of the SPEC-2 (f) rule 5 residue table or converges on retry. A Put creates or overwrites, a Delete of an absent Secret succeeds, and the ETag does not change on a 503. ALTERNATIVES: I did not file the Phase 4 wedge with no writer (a soft-deleted pool keeps its Lenny-written refs, so Phase 4 retries until the Token Service is re-enabled). It fails closed by design (Traps: No-writer erasure).
FACT: the tenant-hash delete-collection is what makes Phase 4 crash-safe. A retry after `CredentialPoolStore` rows are gone still finds the Secrets by label, so a crash between the row delete and the delete-collection leaks nothing while a writer exists. EVIDENCE: spec-changes.md:138, spec/12_storage-architecture.md:926

### [non-spec.1.review-performance.1]
DECISION: filed one finding: the CODE-4 writer's kube client has no stated rate limit, and Tier 3 pool DELETE issues ~1,200 serial Deletes in one request under one row lock — BECAUSE a client built bare (5/10) or from ctrl.GetConfig (20/30) needs 60 to 240 s against lenny-ctl's 30 s default timeout; each timed-out attempt leaves Deletes applied on a still-active pool (rule 5 row 2) and a retry re-issues every Delete — ALTERNATIVES: a pool-hash label plus one deletecollection (spec change, rejected); refiling the probe-under-lock deadline (already refuted as hardening, review-log non-spec.4.review-security.1 and the reliability lens).
FACT: Tier 3 pool sizing is min 1,000 and recommended ~1,200 credentials per pool. EVIDENCE: spec/17_deployment-topology.md:1263-1290
FACT: controller-runtime v0.19.4 ctrl.GetConfig defaults QPS 20 and Burst 30 when they are unset; lenny-ctl's HTTP timeout defaults to 30 s. EVIDENCE: ~/go/pkg/mod/sigs.k8s.io/controller-runtime@v0.19.4/pkg/client/config/config.go:101-105; pkg/ctlcli/main.go:450,568
CORRECTS [non-spec.2.review-performance.1]: an authenticated gitClone source costs up to two keyed reads per session (ref pinning at session creation in workspace_plan.go:78/167, and the clone at bind in archiveextract.go:136), not one. The Tier 3 worst case is therefore ~400 reads/s against 4 x 100 QPS, with no headroom; pick_first gRPC per gateway connection makes the per-replica load uneven. This was not filed, because the share of sessions that carry an authenticated non-SHA gitClone is undocumented. EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/provisioning/vcscred/vcscred.go:3-4
USEFUL [non-spec.2.review-reliability.1]: its probe-under-lock WATCHOUT and the later refutation kept me from refiling it.

### [non-spec.1.review-reliability.1]

DECISION: returned no findings under the reliability lens in non-spec run round 1 — BECAUSE the Phase 4 credential_pool eraser stays idempotent on retry (pgstore DeleteByTenant is a plain DELETE, DeleteTenant is a label DeleteCollection, and tenantEraser aborts and re-runs Phase 4), writer Put (Create then unconditional Update) and Delete (NotFound→nil) are idempotent under client and bootstrap-Job retry, pgtenant.InTx never re-runs the closure (no serialization retry), so a writer call runs at most once per request, and every crash or failover residue is a rule 5 table row — ALTERNATIVES: refiling the gateway-side probe gRPC call with no deadline under the row lock (already refuted as hardening in non-spec.4.review-security.1 and the run-1 reliability pass).
FACT: pgtenant.InTx runs fn once with no retry loop, then Commit; a closure side effect cannot repeat inside one Update call — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/storage/pgtenant/pgtenant.go:76-102
FACT: tenantEraser.DeleteByTenant stops at the first erasing-store error and leaves Phase 4 to re-run every store, relying on each store's idempotence — EVIDENCE: cmd/lenny-gateway/tenantdeletion.go:208-235
WATCHOUT: the gateway HTTP server sets only ReadHeaderTimeout, and probeSecretRefs uses req.Context(), so the in-closure probe is bounded only by the Token Service-side 5 s Get (CODE-2 step 3) and by client disconnect. This was refuted as hardening; do not refile without new evidence — EVIDENCE: cmd/lenny-gateway/httpsurface.go:976-980, pkg/gateway/externalapi/admin/credential_pools.go:363-372

### [non-spec.3.fix-design-G1.1]

FACT: The VCS resolver (`vcscred.Resolver`) has three consumers: `vcsTokenError` (MCP tool path), `podsession` `extractGitCloneSource` (clone at bind), and `sessionserver` `vcsCredentialFunc` (ref pinning at plan binding). Only the last maps a resolver error to a REST code other than `INTERNAL_ERROR`: `resolveRef` wraps every credential-function error as `ResolveAuthFailed`, so it becomes `422 GIT_CLONE_REF_UNRESOLVABLE`. — EVIDENCE: pkg/workspaceplan/refresolve.go `resolveRef` creds branch; pkg/gateway/sessionserver/workspace_plan.go `vcsCredentialFunc`, `writeRefResolveError`; pkg/gateway/podlifecycle/podsession/archiveextract.go `extractGitCloneSource`
WATCHOUT: `pkg/workspaceplan` cannot import `vcscred` or `credentialpoolstore` (`vcscred` imports `workspaceplan`; `go list -deps ./pkg/workspaceplan` shows no internal deps). Classify the credential error in the `sessionserver` closure, which already imports both, and make `resolveRef` keep a `*ResolveError` the credential function returns. Do not put the `ErrNoUsableCredential` test in refresolve.go as the round-3 finding suggested. — EVIDENCE: `go list -deps ./pkg/gateway/provisioning/vcscred` lists pkg/workspaceplan
FACT: Classifying by exclusion is safe at plan binding: `checkGitCloneAuthBindings` runs first and already rejects a malformed leaseScope, a host with no pool, and an ambiguous host, so the remaining non-`ErrNoUsableCredential` resolver errors are the pool-list read and the Token Service read, both transient. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/workspace_plan.go `checkGitCloneAuthBindings`, called before `PinCommitSHAs`
DECISION: Reuse `ResolveNetworkError` / `503 GIT_CLONE_REF_RESOLVE_TRANSIENT` for a transient credential read at plan binding — BECAUSE SPEC-3 (c) rule 3 requires TRANSIENT and this is the only TRANSIENT code on that path. Also, `resolveRef` already returns `ResolveNetworkError` for a non-ls-remote cause ("no ref resolver configured") — ALTERNATIVES: a new reason such as `credential_unavailable` (rejected: a §14.1/§15.1 spec change while the spec loop is at its floor); leave 422 and file a defect (rejected: the proposal's own code would then contradict its staged SPEC-3 rule 3)
OPEN: §15.1 `GIT_CLONE_REF_RESOLVE_TRANSIENT` and §14.1's ls-remote failure sentence list only ls-remote network causes. SPEC-3 (c) rule 3 already makes a plan-binding credential read failure TRANSIENT, and the tree already returns this code with no ls-remote run. A later spec round decides whether to add "or the VCS credential read fails transiently" to the row. The docs mirrors are docs/reference/error-catalog.md and docs/reference/workspace-plan.md. This fix does not cause the gap.

### [non-spec.5.review-performance.1]
CORRECTS [standing Deferred: Token Service QPS/burst]: closed. CODE-2 step 7 now adds `--cluster-qps`/`--cluster-burst` (defaults 100/200) to lenny-token-service and sets cfg.QPS/Burst in `inClusterClientset`; the defaults match the gateway (cmd/lenny-gateway/flags.go:875-878). FACT: Session creation is 200/s at Tier 3 and 2,000/s at Tier 4 (spec/16_observability.md:598). Tier 3 runs 4 Token Service replicas (spec/17_deployment-topology.md:1246), and no Tier 4 replica count is stated. Keyed VCS reads happen only on authenticated gitClone sessions, so the 100 QPS per-replica default covers Tier 3 and is operator-tunable above that. EVIDENCE: spec/16_observability.md:598, spec/17_deployment-topology.md:1246
FACT: The admin `tokenServiceSecretProber` (and so the CODE-6 Resolve on it) does not go through the `token_service` subsystem breaker, which wraps only credassign. VCS read failures therefore cannot trip lease assignment. EVIDENCE: cmd/lenny-gateway/executor_credentials.go:120-139
FACT: buildStores calls buildExecutorAndCredentials (stores.go:77) before buildAuxStores (main.go:139), so `w.tokenServiceConn` exists when CODE-6 step 2 wires vcsCreds. EVIDENCE: cmd/lenny-gateway/stores.go:77, cmd/lenny-gateway/main.go:105,139
DECISION: The performance lens returns no findings this round — BECAUSE the only writes land on the admin path and erasure, nothing touches Redis or etcd status, the lease hot path takes no row lock, and the remaining capacity points (pool DELETE serial Deletes under the lock, the uncached keyed read) are already refuted or owner-settled — ALTERNATIVES: refiling Tier 4 Token Service QPS sizing (rejected: the flags are tunable and no Tier 4 replica budget exists to violate).

### [non-spec.5.review-reliability.1]

FACT: credentialpoolstore pgstore.Update runs the mutate closure exactly once inside pgtenant.InTx, which has no retry loop, so a writer Put/Delete inside the closure is never replayed by the store; retries come only from the client, and Put (Create then unconditional Update) and Delete (NotFound to nil) are idempotent under that. EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/credentials/credentialpoolstore/pgstore/pgstore.go:133-190, pkg/gateway/storage/pgtenant/pgtenant.go:76-102
FACT: credentialpoolstore pgstore.DeleteByTenant is a plain DELETE and is idempotent, so a Phase 4 retry after DeleteByTenant succeeded and DeleteTenant failed reruns both safely. EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/credentials/credentialpoolstore/pgstore/pgstore.go:271-289
FACT: plan binding (PinCommitSHAs) runs before any session state is written, so the new CODE-6 step 3 503 GIT_CLONE_REF_RESOLVE_TRANSIENT is retry-safe on the same path the ls-remote network_error already uses. EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/workspace_plan.go:77-81, :94-98
FACT: buildExecutorAndCredentials (which dials the Token Service) runs from buildStores before main calls buildAuxStores, so CODE-6 step 2 can build vcsCreds from w.tokenServiceConn. EVIDENCE: cmd/lenny-gateway/stores.go:77, cmd/lenny-gateway/main.go:139
DECISION: returned no reliability findings — BECAUSE every crash/failover residue of the admin write pipeline and erasure is a stated rule 5/6 outcome with an idempotent convergence write, and the CODE-6 step 3 reclassification only routes errors already reachable today — ALTERNATIVES: filing the stored `lenny-system/<name>` upgrade break (already UNVERIFIED at review-log:1519, no installed base) and the vcscred unreachable permanent errors (malformed leaseScope, no host) now classed network_error (validateGitClone makes them unreachable).

### [non-spec.5.review-security.1]

- FACT: The plan-binding VCS read is tenant-pinned before the Token Service is reached: `vcsCredentialFunc` binds `s.resolveTenant(r)` and calls `s.vcsCreds.Resolve(ctx, tenantID, ...)`, which lists only that tenant's pools. CODE-6 step 3's new error classification moves no control; an outage gives a retryable 503 with no credential used, so it fails closed. — EVIDENCE: pkg/gateway/sessionserver/workspace_plan.go:158-172, pkg/gateway/provisioning/vcscred/vcscred.go:96-130
- FACT: lenny-ops forwards MCP tool `arguments` to the invoker without logging them, so a `secretData` value sent through `admin.add_credential_to_pool` is not written to lenny-ops logs. The only parse of the arguments reads `operationId`. — EVIDENCE: pkg/ops/mcp/server.go:281-289, :307-317
- FACT: `IncSecretReload` has no production caller, and no alert reads `lenny_token_service_secret_reloads_total`, so CODE-2 step 5's replacement removes no degraded-state signal. — EVIDENCE: pkg/tokenservice/promemit/promemit.go:174-181
- WATCHOUT: In plaintext dev mode the Token Service gRPC surface has no client-cert check, so the keyed read returns values to any caller that can reach it. `AssignCredentials` already returns material on the same surface, so this is not a regression. Do not file it. — EVIDENCE: cmd/lenny-token-service/surfaces.go:94-113
- DECISION: returned no security findings this round — BECAUSE the r5 diff (CODE-6 step 3, SPEC-7 (k), I4, the S18 dependency, the DOCS-1 error-catalog bullet) adds no actor, verb, or bypass, and the pipeline's P5 and P6 cells still close cross-tenant ref injection on every write row, including the seed rows — ALTERNATIVES: considered filing the malformed-leaseScope→TRANSIENT classification (vcscred.go:102,106) but did not, because it is a mechanism or correctness question outside the security lens and it does not fail open.
